    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;

    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

data-custom-css-path="style.css">/* Gerstner Programm - Primary Font */
@font-face {
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/Gerstner-ProgrammRegular.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 400;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

@font-face {
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/Gerstner-ProgrammMedium.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 500;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

/* Apply font family globally with fallbacks */
body, html {
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Oxygen, Ubuntu, Cantarell, sans-serif;
}

/* FT System Mono - Secondary Font (Code) */
@font-face {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/FTSystemMono-Regular.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 400;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

@font-face {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/FTSystemMono-Medium.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 500;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

/* Apply FT System Mono to code elements */
code, pre, .code, [class*="language-"], kbd, samp, tt {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace !important;
}

/* Global heading font weights - make them less bold */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  font-weight: 500 !important; /* medium weight instead of bold/semibold */
}

/* Remove highlighting/backgrounds from card titles and headings */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove any mark/highlight elements from headings */
h1 mark, h2 mark, h3 mark, h4 mark, h5 mark, h6 mark {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  color: inherit !important;
}

/* For extra light headings where explicitly needed */
.font-normal h1,
.font-normal h2,
.font-normal h3 {
  font-weight: 400 !important;
}

/* Consistent border radius for code snippets and cards */
pre,
code,
[id*="code-group"],
.not-prose,
[class*="codeblock"],
[role="tabpanel"],
.mint-card,
[class*="Card"],
.rounded-xl,
.rounded-lg,
.rounded-2xl {
  border-radius: 0.375rem !important; /* 5px equivalent to rounded-md */
}

/* Specific targeting for Mintlify code blocks */
.flex.flex-col.not-prose.relative.overflow-hidden {
  border-radius: 0.375rem !important;
}

/* Override blue colors with Parallel orange */
/* Active tab in code blocks */
[role="tab"][aria-selected="true"],
[role="tab"][data-headlessui-state="selected"] {
  color: #FB631B !important;
  border-color: #FB631B !important;
}

/* Tab border bottom */
[role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] .absolute,
[role="tab"][data-headlessui-state="selected"] .absolute {
  border-color: #FB631B !important;
}

/* Remove any blue backgrounds in dark mode */
.dark .codeblock-dark,
.dark [class*="codeblock"],
.dark pre {
  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;
}

/* Ensure code block backgrounds match theme */
[class*="codeblock"],
.not-prose {
  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;
}

.dark [class*="codeblock"],
.dark .not-prose {
  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;
}

/* Code block container backgrounds */
.bg-white\/60 {
  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;
}

.dark .bg-codeblock,
.dark .codeblock-dark {
  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;
}

#pagination {
  display: none;
}

/* Dark mode: use consistent black background for code sections */
.dark {
  --bg-color: #1d1b16;
}

/* Ensure the code example container has consistent dark background */
.dark .example-panel,
.dark [class*="example-panel"] {
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Remove all button backgrounds in dark mode - outline only */
.dark .example-button {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Active button gets orange background */
.dark .example-button.text-white {
  background-color: #fb631b !important;
}

/* Remove backgrounds from code block containers and cards in dark mode - outline only */
.dark [class*="Card"],
.dark .mint-card,
.dark a[href] > div {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove hover backgrounds on card titles and content */
.dark [class*="Card"]:hover,
.dark .mint-card:hover,
.dark a[href]:hover > div,
.dark a[href] > div:hover {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove background from code section container */
.dark .rounded-md.border {
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Remove any highlighting from card containers and titles in light mode too */
[class*="Card"],
.mint-card,
[class*="Card"] h1,
[class*="Card"] h2,
[class*="Card"] h3,
[class*="Card"] h4 {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove card container backgrounds completely */
[class*="Card"] > div,
.mint-card > div,
[role="group"] > a > div {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove highlight from card title elements */
[class*="Card"] [class*="title"],
.mint-card [class*="title"] {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Style Resource card titles to match the top cards */
[class*="Card"] h3,
[class*="Card"] h4,
.mint-card h3,
.mint-card h4 {
  font-size: 1.125rem !important; /* text-lg */
  font-weight: 600 !important; /* font-semibold */
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem !important; /* mb-2 */
}

/* Aggressive removal of any highlighting on all elements - but NOT in code blocks */
*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*="language-"]):not(.token) {
  text-decoration-color: inherit !important;
}

/* Remove any search highlights or selection backgrounds that might appear - but NOT in code */
mark:not(pre mark):not(code mark),
::selection:not(pre ::selection):not(code ::selection) {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Remove highlight from any inline code or special elements - but exclude code blocks */
*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*="language-"]):not(.token)::before,
*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*="language-"]):not(.token)::after {
  background: inherit !important;
}

/* Target Mintlify-specific card components */
[data-card], [data-card-title], .card, .card-title {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove any underline decorations that might look like highlights */
a[href] h1, a[href] h2, a[href] h3, a[href] h4 {
  text-decoration: none !important;
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Increase main navigation tabs font size (Home, Documentation, API Reference, Changelog) */
header nav button,
header [role="navigation"] a,
header [role="navigation"] button,
[data-tabs] a,
[data-tabs] button,
nav[data-orientation="horizontal"] a,
nav[data-orientation="horizontal"] button {
  font-size: 1.25rem !important; /* 20px - equivalent to text-xl */
}

/* Fix list bullets - scope to article content only, exclude navigation */
article ul:not(nav ul):not([role="navigation"] ul),
.prose ul:not(nav ul):not([role="navigation"] ul) {
  list-style-type: disc;
  list-style-position: outside;
  padding-left: 1.5rem; /* Adjust bullet indent */
}

article ol:not(nav ol):not([role="navigation"] ol),
.prose ol:not(nav ol):not([role="navigation"] ol) {
  list-style-type: decimal;
  list-style-position: outside;
  padding-left: 1.5rem;
}

article li:not(nav li):not([role="navigation"] li),
.prose li:not(nav li):not([role="navigation"] li) {
  display: list-item;
  margin-left: 0.5rem; /* Fine-tune bullet spacing */
}

/* Explicitly hide bullets in navigation/sidebar.
   Only remove the list marker here — do NOT force `display` on these
   elements. Mintlify's navbar renders the primary button as a responsive
   pair (a `lg:hidden` text link + a `hidden lg:flex` button) and relies on
   those utilities to show only one per breakpoint. Since this stylesheet is
   unlayered, a `display: block` here beats Mintlify's layered Tailwind
   utilities and makes BOTH variants show at once (duplicate header button). */
nav ul,
nav ol,
nav li,
[role="navigation"] ul,
[role="navigation"] ol,
[role="navigation"] li,
aside ul,
aside ol,
aside li {
  list-style-type: none !important;
}

/* Let content use the full layout width on pages without a right-hand TOC.
   Mintlify caps #content-container at 864px (max-w-216) when no TOC renders,
   leaving the TOC gutter empty and the page lopsided on wide screens. Pages
   with a TOC are unaffected: flexbox already bounds the content column there. */
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  #content-container {
    max-width: none !important;
  }
}

/* Give billing details more room without changing other documentation tables. */
.pricing-summary th:first-child {
  width: 28%;
}

.pricing-summary th:nth-child(2) {
  width: 48%;
}

/* Home page: cap prompt panel height and scroll inside */
.example-panel pre {
  max-height: 340px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  word-break: break-word;
  scrollbar-gutter: stable;
}

/* Home page: hide Mintlify's built-in code block toolbar inside example panels.
   The toolbar is two sibling divs at top-right of each `.code-block`, both with
   a `print:hidden` class: one holds the Copy + Ask-AI buttons, the other is a
   white fade gradient that paints behind them. Hiding only the buttons left the
   white gradient showing as a "mystery white square" next to the scrollbar.
   We use our own Copy-prompt button instead. */
.example-panel .code-block > .print\:hidden {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Home page: scrollbar flush to the right edge of the prompt panel */
.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 8px;
}
.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);
  border-radius: 4px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  background-clip: padding-box;
}
.dark .example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);
  background-clip: padding-box;
}
.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: transparent;
}

/* Home page: vertical tab styling for the prompts picker (Figma spec —
   gray active state instead of orange tint). */
.example-tab-active {
  background-color: #eeeeee !important;
}
.dark .example-tab-active {
  background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important;
}

/* Reset Mintlify's `.prose ul/li` styles for our tab list. The global
   prose rules add padding-left: 1.5rem to ul, margin-left: 0.5rem to li,
   and `display: list-item` (which can add internal padding-left). All of
   that pushes the tab buttons ~64px right of the column edge. */
.example-tab-list,
.example-tab-list li {
  padding: 0 !important;
  margin: 0 !important;
  list-style: none !important;
}
.example-tab-list li { display: block !important; }

/* Drop the prose-injected mt-5/mb-8 on Mintlify code blocks inside our
   prompt panels so the code block fits flush inside the gray surround
   with just the wrapper's p-2 visible as inset. */
.example-panel .code-block {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
}

/* Home page: panel doc links */
.home-panel-link {
  font-size: 0.875rem;
  color: #4b5563;
  transition: color 0.15s;
}

.home-panel-link:hover {
  color: #FB631B;
}

.dark .home-panel-link {
  color: #9ca3af;
}

.dark .home-panel-link:hover {
  color: #FB631B;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Home page: backfill arbitrary Tailwind classes that Mintlify's static CSS
   tree-shakes (only ones used on this page). Without these, the home renders
   for ~2.8s with the wrong dimensions (full-width tab column, stacked MCP
   tiles, no padding) and snaps into place once runtime Tailwind hydrates —
   visible as a "pop-in" of the tab list and the MCP tile row.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/* Min-heights reserve layout space so first paint matches final paint. */
.min-h-\[260px\] { min-height: 260px; }

/* Custom colors used in the prompts picker + MCP tiles + panel header bar. */
.bg-\[\#f6f6f6\] { background-color: #f6f6f6; }
.bg-\[\#434343\] { background-color: #434343; }
.hover\:bg-\[\#2a2a2a\]:hover { background-color: #2a2a2a; }
.border-\[\#e5e5e5\] { border-color: #e5e5e5; }
.dark .dark\:bg-\[\#1a1a1a\] { background-color: #1a1a1a; }
.dark .dark\:border-\[\#2a2a2a\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }
.dark .dark\:bg-\[\#111\] { background-color: #111; }

/* Tile + button micro-interactions. */
.bg-gray-50\/60 { background-color: rgb(249 250 251 / 0.6); }
.dark .dark\:bg-white\/\[0\.02\] { background-color: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.02); }

/* Text sizes used on MCP tiles + panel header. */
.text-\[12px\] { font-size: 12px; }
.text-\[13px\] { font-size: 13px; }

/* Responsive (lg+) layout for the prompts picker + MCP grid. Without these
   the tab column fills the full width and the MCP tiles stack 2-up instead
   of 4-up on desktop. */
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  .lg\:w-\[262px\] { width: 262px; }
  .lg\:p-5 { padding: 1.25rem; }
  .lg\:grid-cols-4 { grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
  .lg\:border-r { border-right-width: 1px; }
  .lg\:border-b-0 { border-bottom-width: 0; }
  .lg\:border-gray-200 { border-color: rgb(229 231 235); }
  .dark .lg\:dark\:border-\[\#2a2a2a\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Overview page only: lift the *inner* content cap added by `mode: "wide"`.
   The outer `max-w-8xl` cap (1472px, also used by every other docs page) is
   kept so the sidebar stays in its normal position and the article column
   sits in the same total band as the rest of the docs. The inner cap added
   by wide-mode (`peer-[.is-wide]:[&>div:last-child]:max-w-6xl`, 1152px) is
   what was squishing the content into the leftmost ~1100px and leaving a
   big empty area where the right TOC normally lives. Removing only that
   one lets the page fill the full sidebar-to-TOC band without breaking the
   rest of the layout. Scoped via `data-current-path` on <html>.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] [class*="max-w-6xl"] > div:last-child {
  max-width: none !important;
}

/* Overview page only: hide Mintlify's auto-generated page-header block
   (eyebrow + h1 title + description + Copy-page menu). The page is built
   as a custom landing experience that starts directly with the
   "Install the Parallel Search MCP" section, so the auto title is
   redundant. The whole block lives inside <header id="header">. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] header#header {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Overview page only: section headings ("Install the Parallel Search MCP",
   "Build with the Parallel APIs") should match the docs h1 weight. The
   global rule above forces all h*-tags to weight 500; override that just
   for headings that opt-in via the `.font-bold` Tailwind class on this
   page so they read at h1 weight without affecting any other page. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] h2.font-bold {
  font-weight: 700 !important;
}

/* Overview page only: zero out the prose-injected top margins that were
   designed to space content below the (now hidden) page header. Without
   these resets, the first heading sits ~90px below the top nav purely
   from stacked margins (h2 margin-top:60px from prose + mdx-content
   margin-top:32px from `mt-8`). The wrapper divs in the page already
   handle inter-section spacing, so we don't need any of this. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .mdx-content {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
}
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .mdx-content h2 {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
}

/* Overview page only: onboarding prompt body should match the
   Build-with-Parallel-APIs prompt panels — FT System Mono at the same
   13px Shiki size used inside .example-panel pre, normal weight. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt code {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace !important;
  font-size: 14px !important;
  font-weight: 400 !important;
  line-height: 1.6 !important;
}

/* Onboarding prompt link: match the shade of blue used for markdown
   `#` headings in the prompt panels below (rgb(5, 80, 174)). Tailwind
   arbitrary values like text-[#2563eb] get tree-shaken from Mintlify's
   static CSS bundle, so set color + weight here so they actually land. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:hover,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:focus,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:active,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:visited {
  color: rgb(5, 80, 174) !important;
  font-weight: 400 !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  border-bottom: 0 !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:hover,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:focus,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:active,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:visited,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:hover,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:focus,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:active,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:visited {
  color: #79c0ff !important;
}

/* Content links: the underline is enough — no bold/semibold weight */
.prose a:not([class*="card"]):not([class*="button"]) {
  font-weight: 400 !important;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Cookie consent banner. Dark frosted card, bottom-left on desktop,   */
/* bottom sheet on mobile. Matches parallel.ai's banner, which is      */
/* deliberately identical in light and dark mode.                      */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

.pw-consent {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: 1rem;
  left: 1rem;
  right: 1rem;
  z-index: 2147483000;
  max-height: calc(100dvh - 2rem);
  overflow-y: auto;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: rgba(34, 34, 34, 0.8);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  padding: 1rem;
  color: #fff;
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
  opacity: 0;
  transform: translateY(12px);
  transition: opacity 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.6, 0, 0.2, 1), transform 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.6, 0, 0.2, 1);
}

.pw-consent[data-visible="true"] {
  opacity: 1;
  transform: translateY(0);
}

@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .pw-consent {
    bottom: 1.5rem;
    left: 1.5rem;
    right: auto;
    width: 300px;
  }
}

.pw-consent__stack {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 1rem;
}

.pw-consent__title {
  font-size: 16px;
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 24px;
  letter-spacing: 0.15px;
  margin: 0;
  color: #fff;
}

.pw-consent__body {
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 16px;
  letter-spacing: 0.15px;
  margin: 0;
  color: #fff;
}

.pw-consent__body a,
.pw-consent__linkbtn {
  color: #fff;
  text-decoration: underline;
  background: none;
  border: 0;
  padding: 0;
  font: inherit;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.pw-consent__panel {
  display: none;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.75rem;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
  padding-top: 1rem;
}

.pw-consent__panel[data-expanded="true"] {
  display: flex;
}

.pw-consent__row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.75rem;
}

.pw-consent__label {
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 16px;
  letter-spacing: 0.15px;
  color: #fff;
}

.pw-consent__hint {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 2px;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 15px;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55);
}

.pw-consent__always {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  margin-top: 2px;
  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace;
  font-size: 11px;
  line-height: 16px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55);
}

.pw-consent__toggle {
  position: relative;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  margin-top: 2px;
  width: 34px;
  height: 20px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 9999px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: background-color 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.6, 0, 0.2, 1);
}

.pw-consent__toggle[aria-checked="true"] {
  background: #fff;
}

.pw-consent__knob {
  position: absolute;
  top: 2px;
  left: 2px;
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  border-radius: 9999px;
  background: #fff;
  transition: left 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.6, 0, 0.2, 1), background-color 0.2s;
}

.pw-consent__toggle[aria-checked="true"] .pw-consent__knob {
  left: 16px;
  background: #222;
}

.pw-consent__actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 1rem;
}

.pw-consent__btn {
  flex: 1 1 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  white-space: nowrap;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: #eee;
  color: #181818;
  padding: 8px 16px;
  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 16px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: background-color 0.2s;
}

.pw-consent__btn:hover {
  background: #fff;
}

.pw-consent__btn--save {
  margin-top: 4px;
  width: 100%;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .pw-consent,
  .pw-consent__toggle,
  .pw-consent__knob,
  .pw-consent__btn {
    transition: none;
  }
}

class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Data Connectors" data-page-href="/resources/data-connectors" id="content"><div class="mint-sr-only" aria-hidden="false"><span data-as="p">For AI agents: a documentation index is available at <a href="https://docs.parallel.ai/llms.txt" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">https://docs.parallel.ai/llms.txt</a>. The full text of all docs is at <a href="https://docs.parallel.ai/llms-full.txt" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">https://docs.parallel.ai/llms-full.txt</a>. You may also fetch any page as Markdown by appending <code>.md</code> to its URL or sending <code>Accept: text/markdown</code>.</span><span data-as="p">For Search and Extract, use <code>/v1/search</code> and <code>/v1/extract</code> for all new integrations and agent tool calls. Use <code>/v1beta/search</code> and <code>/v1beta/extract</code> only when maintaining an existing integration that already uses them. The request bodies changed too: on <code>/v1/extract</code>, <code>full_content</code>, <code>fetch_policy</code> and <code>excerpt_settings</code> go inside <code>advanced_settings</code>, and sending the v1beta top-level <code>excerpts</code>, <code>full_content</code> and <code>fetch_policy</code> to <code>/v1/extract</code> returns a 422. Do not substitute <code>/v1</code> for the documented FindAll or Ingest endpoint paths.</span></div>
<span data-as="p">Data Connectors let a Task Run or a Responses API request combine web research with specialized data from third-party providers. Browse what is available in the <a href="https://parallel.ai/data-connectors" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Data Connectors directory</a>. The right access model depends on how the data is licensed and made available to your organization.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_19bb9qnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Access model</th><th>Best for</th><th>How you use it</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Index Partners</strong></td><td>Data Parallel has licensed for every eligible run</td><td>Ask your question normally. Included by default, with nothing to configure.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Pay-Per-Use</strong></td><td>A connector you want Parallel to query for a specific run</td><td>Name the connector in <code>advanced_settings.data_sources</code>, under <code>free</code> or <code>pay_per_use</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Bring Your Own License (BYOL)</strong></td><td>Data your organization already licenses or hosts</td><td>Add the provider’s remote MCP server and your credentials to <code>mcp_servers</code>.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">All three complement Parallel’s default web research. Pay-Per-Use connectors and BYOL servers are added to the run; they do not replace web search and planning.</span>
<span data-as="p">The field names above are the Task API’s. The Responses API takes the same connector names in a top-level <code>data_sources</code> field and BYOL servers as OpenAI <code>mcp</code> tools; see <a href="#responses-api" class="link">Responses API</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="index-partners"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#index-partners" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Index Partners</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Index Partners are licensed by Parallel through <a href="https://index.parallel.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Index</a> and are included by default on every eligible run. Use the Task API normally, and the processor draws on them when they are relevant. There is nothing to configure, and nothing to select: naming an Index Partner in <code>data_sources</code> is rejected.</span>
<span data-as="p">Availability varies by partner and by processor.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="pay-per-use"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#pay-per-use" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Pay-Per-Use</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Pay-Per-Use connectors are invoked explicitly, per run. Select them with the <code>advanced_settings.data_sources</code> field when creating a Task Run. The processor queries them the same way it queries the web: it decides when a connector is useful for the task, calls it, and cites what it used in the <code>basis</code>. You do not host anything or manage credentials.</span>
<span data-as="p">Free connectors, such as PubMed and ClinicalTrials.gov, use the same explicit invocation model and are never billed. Paid connectors are billed per successful call.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_5pbb9qnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>free</code></td><td><code>array[string]</code></td><td>Free connectors to enable for this run. Never billed.</td></tr><tr><td><code>pay_per_use</code></td><td><code>array[string]</code></td><td>Pay-per-use connectors to enable for this run. Each successful call the processor makes is billed.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Both lists are optional and empty by default.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="pay-per-use-connectors"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#pay-per-use-connectors" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Pay-per-use connectors</span></h3>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_99bb9qnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Covers</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>carbonarc</code></td><td>Carbon Arc: alternative data on companies and markets, including consumer spend, foot traffic, app and web activity, and events.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="billing"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#billing" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Billing</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Pay-per-use connectors are billed through Parallel at a price per call set by the provider. Charges are based on successful connector tool calls and are separate from the Task processor charge. Free connectors are never billed.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="how-pay-per-use-connectors-are-used"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#how-pay-per-use-connectors-are-used" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">How Pay-Per-Use connectors are used</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">When a run starts, Parallel attaches the selected connectors as tools. The processor calls a connector only when it judges it relevant to the task, so a selected connector may be called several times or not at all.</span>
<span data-as="p">Every call appears in the run output’s <code>mcp_tool_calls</code> list with <code>server_name</code> set to the connector name, using the same shape as <a class="link" href="/task-api/mcp-tool-call#response-content">MCP tool calls</a>. Facts drawn from a connector are cited in the <code>basis</code> like any other source.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="restrictions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#restrictions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Restrictions</span></h3>
<ul>
<li>Pay-Per-Use connectors are supported on the Task API <a class="link" href="/task-api/guides/choose-a-processor">processors</a> (<code>lite</code>, <code>base</code>, <code>core</code>, <code>core2x</code>, <code>pro</code>, <code>ultra</code>, <code>ultra2x</code>, <code>ultra4x</code>, <code>ultra8x</code>). Selecting one on an unsupported processor is rejected.</li>
<li>Each connector belongs to one list. Naming a free connector under <code>pay_per_use</code>, or a paid connector under <code>free</code>, is rejected.</li>
<li>A connector name may not match the <code>name</code> of a server in <code>mcp_servers</code> on the same request.</li>
<li>Connectors are being rolled out per organization. If a selected connector is not yet available to your organization, the request is rejected with a <code>422</code> whose message lists the connectors you can use. Contact <a href="mailto:support@parallel.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">support@parallel.ai</a> to request access.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="bring-your-own-license-byol"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#bring-your-own-license-byol" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Bring Your Own License (BYOL)</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Use BYOL when your organization already holds a license to a provider or operates its own private data service. Pass the provider’s remote MCP server in <code>mcp_servers</code>, including any authentication headers required by that server. The licenses you already hold carry straight into your Task Runs.</span>

<span data-as="p">Parallel discovers the server’s available tools and lets the processor call them when they are useful for the task. See <a class="link" href="/task-api/mcp-tool-call">MCP Tool Calling</a> for authentication, configuration fields, restrictions, and complete request and response examples.</span>

<span data-as="p">The <a class="link" href="/responses-api/responses-quickstart">Responses API</a> selects connectors with a top-level <code>data_sources</code> field that takes the same <code>free</code> and <code>pay_per_use</code> lists and connector names as the Task API. The same billing applies.</span>

<span data-as="p"><code>data_sources</code> is a Parallel extension, so the OpenAI Python SDK sends it through <code>extra_body</code>. The TypeScript SDK sends unknown fields as they are.</span>
<ul>
<li>Connectors in <code>data_sources</code> require <code>reasoning.effort</code> <code>medium</code> (the default) or <code>high</code>. Selecting one with <code>low</code> is rejected.</li>
<li>Every call to a connector in <code>data_sources</code> is returned as an <code>mcp_call</code> output item with <code>server_label</code> set to the connector name, the same item your own MCP tools produce. Calls to Index Partners are not reported.</li>
<li>For BYOL, pass the provider’s server as an OpenAI <code>mcp</code> tool. See <a class="link" href="/responses-api/features/mcp-tools">MCP Tools</a> for the fields, restrictions, and output. A connector name may not match the <code>server_label</code> of an <code>mcp</code> tool on the same request.</li>
<li>Rejected requests return a <code>400</code> in the OpenAI error envelope, not a <code>422</code>. The unavailable-connector message is the same and lists the connectors you can use.</li>
<li>The earlier <code>additional_data_providers</code> field is retired, and a request that sends it is rejected with a message pointing to <code>data_sources</code>.</li>
</ul>

web APIs (Search, Extract, Task/Deep Research, Responses, FindAll, and Monitor) give AI agents and developers high-quality, low-latency access to the web for research, content extraction, entity discovery, and continuous monitoring. For Search and Extract, use /v1/search and /v1/extract for all new integrations and agent tool calls; use /v1beta/search and /v1beta/extract only when maintaining an existing integration that already uses them. The request bodies changed too: on /v1/extract, full_content, fetch_policy and excerpt_settings go inside advanced_settings, and sending the v1beta top-level excerpts, full_content and fetch_policy to /v1/extract returns a 422. Do not substitute /v1 for the documented FindAll or Ingest endpoint */\npre,\ncode,\n[id*=\"code-group\"],\n.not-prose,\n[class*=\"codeblock\"],\n[role=\"tabpanel\"],\n.mint-card,\n[class*=\"Card\"],\n.rounded-xl,\n.rounded-lg,\n.rounded-2xl {\n  border-radius: {\n  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;\n}\n\n/* Code block container backgrounds */\n.bg-white\\/60 {\n  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;\n}\n\n.dark .bg-codeblock,\n.dark .codeblock-dark {\n  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;\n}\n\n#pagination {\n  display: none;\n}\n\n/* Dark mode: use consistent black background for code sections */\n.dark {\n  --bg-color: #1d1b16;\n}\n\n/* Ensure */\n*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*=\"language-\"]):not(.token) {\n  text-decoration-color: inherit !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any search highlights or selection backgrounds that might appear - but !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any underline decorations that might look like highlights */\na[href] h1, a[href] h2, a[href] h3, a[href] h4 {\n  text-decoration: none !important;\n  background: none Since this stylesheet is\n   unlayered, a `display: block` here beats Mintlify's layered Tailwind\n   utilities and makes BOTH variants show at once (duplicate header button). */\nnav ul,\nnav ol,\nnav li,\n[role=\"navigation\"] ul,\n[role=\"navigation\"] ol,\n[role=\"navigation\"] li,\naside ul,\naside ol,\naside li {\n  list-style-type: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Let content use the full layout width on pages without a right-hand TOC.\n   Mintlify caps #content-container at 864px (max-w-216) when no TOC renders,\n   leaving the TOC gutter empty and the page lopsided on wide screens. Pages\n   with a TOC are unaffected: flexbox already bounds the content column there. */\n@media (min-width: 1024px) {\n  #content-container {\n    max-width: none !important;\n  }\n}\n\n/* Give billing details more room without changing other documentation tables. */\n.pricing-summary th:first-child {\n  width: 28%;\n}\n\n.pricing-summary th:nth-child(2) {\n  width: 48%;\n}\n\n/* Home page: cap prompt panel height and scroll inside */\n.example-panel pre {\n  max-height: 340px;\n  overflow-y: auto;\n  overflow-x: hidden;\n  white-space: pre-wrap;\n  word-break: break-word;\n  scrollbar-gutter: stable;\n}\n\n/* Home page: hide Mintlify's built-in code block toolbar inside example panels.\n   The toolbar is two sibling divs at top-right of each `.code-block`, both with\n   a `print:hidden` class: one holds the Copy + Ask-AI buttons, the other is a\n   white fade gradient that paints behind them. Hiding only the buttons left the\n   white gradient showing as a \"mystery white square\" next to the scrollbar.\n   We use our own Copy-prompt button instead. */\n.example-panel .code-block \u003e .print\\:hidden {\n  display: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Home page: scrollbar flush to the right edge of the prompt panel */\n.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar {\n  width: 8px;\n}\n.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {\n  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  background-clip: padding-box;\n}\n.dark .example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {\n  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);\n  background-clip: padding-box;\n}\n.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-track {\n  background: transparent;\n}\n\n/* Home page: vertical tab styling for the prompts picker (Figma spec —\n   gray active state instead of orange tint). */\n.example-tab-active {\n  background-color: #eeeeee !important;\n}\n.dark .example-tab-active {\n  background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important;\n}\n\n/* Reset Mintlify's `.prose ul/li` styles for our tab list. All of\n   that pushes the tab buttons ~64px right of the column edge. */\n.example-tab-list,\n.example-tab-list li {\n  padding: 0 !important;\n  margin: 0 !important;\n  list-style: none !important;\n}\n.example-tab-list li { display: block !important; }\n\n/* Drop the prose-injected mt-5/mb-8 on Mintlify code blocks inside our\n   prompt panels so the code block fits flush inside the gray surround\n   with just the wrapper's p-2 visible as inset. */\n.example-panel .code-block {\n  margin-top: 0 !important;\n  margin-bottom: 0 !important;\n}\n\n/* Home page: panel doc links */\n.home-panel-link {\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  color: #4b5563;\n  transition: color 0.15s;\n}\n\n.home-panel-link:hover {\n  color: #FB631B;\n}\n\n.dark .home-panel-link {\n  color: #9ca3af;\n}\n\n.dark .home-panel-link:hover {\n  color: #FB631B;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   Home page: backfill arbitrary Tailwind classes that Mintlify's static CSS\n   tree-shakes (only ones used on this page). Without these\n   the tab column fills the full width and the MCP tiles stack 2-up instead\n   of 4-up on desktop. */\n@media (min-width: 1024px) {\n  .lg\\:w-\\[262px\\] { width: 262px; }\n  .lg\\:p-5 { padding: 1.25rem; }\n  .lg\\:grid-cols-4 { grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); }\n  .lg\\:border-r { border-right-width: 1px; }\n  .lg\\:border-b-0 { border-bottom-width: 0; }\n  .lg\\:border-gray-200 { border-color: rgb(229 231 235); }\n  .dark .lg\\:dark\\:border-\\[\\#2a2a2a\\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   Overview page only: lift the *inner* content cap added by `mode: \"wide\"`.\n   The outer `max-w-8xl` cap (1472px, also used by every other docs page) is\n   kept so the sidebar stays in its normal position and the article column\n   sits in the same total band as the rest of the docs. The whole block lives inside \u003cheader id=\"header\"\u003e. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] header#header {\n  display: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Overview page only: section headings (\"Install the Parallel Search MCP\",\n   \"Build with the Parallel APIs\") should match the docs h1 weight. The\n   global rule above forces all h*-tags to weight 500; override that just\n   for headings that opt-in via the `.font-bold` Tailwind class on this\n   page so they read at h1 weight without affecting any other page. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] h2.font-bold {\n  font-weight: 700 !important;\n}\n\n/* Overview page only: zero out the prose-injected top margins that were\n   designed to space content below the (now hidden) page header. * Cookie consent and consent-gated PostHog for docs.parallel.ai.\n *\n * Mintlify injects every .js file in this repo into every page, so this runs\n * site-wide, after the page becomes interactive. It is served as readable\n * source, not a compiled asset: keep nothing here you would not publish.\n *\n * Consent is shared with parallel.ai through cookies scoped\n * Domain=.parallel.ai, so a visitor is asked once across both sites:\n *   pp_region  - \"unregulated\" | \"regulated\". READ-ONLY here; docs has no geo.\n *   pp_consent - JSON {analytics, marketing, v, ts}\n *\n * UMD rather than an ES module, so it is valid however Mintlify injects it.\n */\n(function (root, factory) {\n  var api = factory();\n  if (typeof module === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 module.exports) {\n    module.exports = api;\n  } else {\n    root.ParallelConsent = api;\n  }\n})(typeof globalThis !== \"undefined\" ? globalThis : self, function () {\n  \"use strict\";\n\n  // Layer 1: core.
